1025: def registrable_column(header, page_txt):
1026:     """从表头串里取出**在正文中连续出现、且语义与整段一致**的列名。
1027: 
1028:     第十九轮 D2：登记值必须同时满足两条——①是原件里的连续片段（否则引文里
1029:     找不到它）；②`canon_column` 与整段表头**完全相同**（否则等于用一个更短的、
1030:     丢了区间或相对年份的名字去登记）。两条都满足才返回。
1031:     """
1032:     want = canon_column(header)
1033:     if not want:
1034:         return ""
1035:     cands = [clean_column_text(header)]
1036:     cands += [m.group(0) for m in _COL_TOKEN.finditer(_nz(header))]
1037:     for c in cands:
1038:         if c and c in (page_txt or "") and canon_column(c) == want:
1039:             return c
1040:     return ""
1041: 
1042: 
1043: def gap(ent, expect, evidence_of, col_of=None):
1044:     """返回该记录不足以证明兼容的原因；None 表示依据充分。
1045: 
1046:     `expect` = {"field": 指标ID, "p1": {...}, "p2": {...}}，每侧含
1047:     doc / period / value / consolidation_scope / unit_scale / currency /
1048:     adjust_version，取自 B 的实际父输入。
1049:     `evidence_of(doc_id, page)` 返回该文档**该物理页**的原文（可为空串）。
1050:     """
1051:     if ent is None:
1052:         return "NO_VERIFICATION_RECORD"
1053:     if (ent.get("conclusion") or "").strip() != "COMPATIBLE":
1054:         return "VERIFICATION_NOT_COMPATIBLE"
1055:     if not _reviewer_ok(ent):
1056:         return "VERIFICATION_WITHOUT_REVIEWER"
1057:     if not _role_ok(ent):
1058:         return "VERIFICATION_WITHOUT_ROLE"
1059:     if not _date_ok(ent.get("reviewed_at")):
1060:         return "VERIFICATION_WITHOUT_DATE"
1061: 
1062:     # 绑定实际父输入：文档 / 期间 / 终值 / 栏目 / 页码 / 版本
1063:     aw_of = {}
1064:     for side in ("p1", "p2"):
1065:         exp = expect[side]
1066:         if (ent.get("%s_doc" % side) or "").strip() != (exp.get("doc") or "").strip():
1067:             return "PARENT_DOC_MISMATCH"
1068:         if (ent.get("%s_period" % side) or "").strip() != (exp.get("period") or "").strip():
1069:             return "PARENT_PERIOD_MISMATCH"
1070:         if (ent.get("%s_value" % side) or "").strip() != (exp.get("value") or "").strip():
1071:             return "PARENT_VALUE_MISMATCH"
1072:         col = _nz(ent.get("%s_column" % side))
1073:         if not col:
1074:             return "PARENT_COLUMN_EMPTY"
1075:         if len(col) < 2:
1076:             return "PARENT_COLUMN_TOO_SHORT"
1077:         # 第十九轮 D2：改用 `canon_column` 判「是不是期间列名」。它要求表头带
1078:         # 年份/区间/相对年/累计属性之一，比 `_PERIOD_COL` 的裸子串更严——
1079:         # `_PERIOD_COL` 里「月」一个字的线索就能过。
1080:         if not canon_column(col):
1081:             return "PARENT_COLUMN_NOT_PERIOD"
1082:         if not _page_ok(ent.get("%s_page" % side)):
1083:             return "PARENT_PAGE_INVALID"
1084:         # 栏目必须真是该金额所在列（第十一轮 B 项）：只验「列头出现在引文里」
1085:         # 挡不住「金额在本期列、登记写上期列」。`col_of` 由调用方按原件词坐标提供，
1086:         # 返回 (column_text, status)。第十二轮再收紧：绑定目标行、期间类比对、
1087:         # 扫描页记「待回读」而不是「原件不可解」。
1088:         if col_of is not None:
1089:             res = col_of((ent.get("%s_doc" % side) or "").strip(),
1090:                          (ent.get("%s_page" % side) or "").strip(),
1091:                          exp.get("value"),
1092:                          METRIC_ALIASES.get((expect.get("field") or "").strip()))
1093:             actual_col, st = res[0], res[1]
1094:             aw_of[side] = res[2] if len(res) > 2 else None
1095:             if st == "NO_WORDS":
1096:                 return "PARENT_COLUMN_NEEDS_REREAD"
1097:             if st == "AMBIGUOUS":
1098:                 return "PARENT_COLUMN_AMBIGUOUS"
1099:             if st == "AMOUNT_PRECISION_INVALID":
1100:                 return "PARENT_AMOUNT_PRECISION_INVALID"
1101:             if st == "TABLE_BOUNDARY_UNPROVEN":
1102:                 return "PARENT_TABLE_BOUNDARY_UNPROVEN"
1103:             if not actual_col:
1104:                 return "PARENT_COLUMN_UNRESOLVED"
1105:             if not cols_equivalent(col, actual_col):
1106:                 return "PARENT_COLUMN_MISMATCH"
1107:         ver = (ent.get("%s_adjust_version" % side) or "").strip()
1108:         if ver not in ADJUST_VERSIONS:
1109:             return "PARENT_ADJUST_VERSION_INVALID"
1110:         actual = ver_class(exp.get("adjust_version"))
1111:         if actual is None:
1112:             return "PARENT_ACTUAL_VERSION_UNKNOWN"
1113:         if ver != actual:
1114:             return "PARENT_ADJUST_VERSION_MISMATCH"
1115: 
1116:     # 两父必须取自同一调整版本基础
1117:     v1 = (ent.get("p1_adjust_version") or "").strip()
1118:     v2 = (ent.get("p2_adjust_version") or "").strip()
1119:     if v1 != v2:
1120:         return "PARENT_ADJUST_VERSION_INCONSISTENT"
1121:     if ver_class(expect["p1"].get("adjust_version")) != \
1122:             ver_class(expect["p2"].get("adjust_version")):
1123:         return "PARENT_ACTUAL_VERSION_INCONSISTENT"
1124: 
1125:     # 口径：非空 + 与实际父输入一致 + 有依据
1126:     for f in ("consolidation_scope", "unit_scale", "currency"):
1127:         v = (ent.get(f) or "").strip()
1128:         if not v:
1129:             return "SCOPE_FIELD_EMPTY"
1130:         if v != (expect["p1"].get(f) or "").strip():
1131:             return "SCOPE_MISMATCH_WITH_PARENT"
1132:     if (ent.get("unit_scale") or "").strip() not in AMOUNT_UNITS:
1133:         return "SCOPE_UNIT_NOT_AMOUNT"
1134:     if len(_nz(ent.get("scope_basis"))) < _MIN_BASIS:
1135:         return "SCOPE_BASIS_MISSING"
1136: 
1137:     names = METRIC_ALIASES.get((expect.get("field") or "").strip())
1138:     # 引文：够长、在该页可定位、含目标指标 + 期间列头 + 父数 + 版本标记
1139:     for side, fq, fd, fp in (("p1", "p1_quote", "p1_doc", "p1_page"),
1140:                              ("p2", "p2_quote", "p2_doc", "p2_page")):
1141:         q = _nz(ent.get(fq))
1142:         if len(q) < _MIN_QUOTE:
1143:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_TOO_SHORT"
1144:         doc = (ent.get(fd) or "").strip()
1145:         src = _nz(evidence_of(doc, (ent.get(fp) or "").strip()))
1146:         if not src:
1147:             return "PARENT_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE"
1148:         if q not in src:
1149:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_NOT_LOCATED"
1150:         if names and not any(n in q for n in names):
1151:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_WITHOUT_TARGET_METRIC"
1152:         if _nz(ent.get("%s_column" % side)) not in q:
1153:             return "PARENT_COLUMN_NOT_IN_QUOTE"
1154:         if (ent.get("%s_adjust_version" % side) or "").strip() == "RESTATED":
1155:             if not any(mk in q for mk in _VER_MARK_RESTATED):
1156:                 return "PARENT_QUOTE_WITHOUT_VERSION_MARK"
1157:         else:
1158:             if any(mk in q for mk in _VER_MARK_FORBIDDEN_ORIGINAL):
1159:                 return "PARENT_QUOTE_VERSION_CONFLICT"
1160:         if not _num_in_quote(ent.get(fq), expect[side].get("value"),
1161:                              expect[side].get("unit_scale"),
1162:                              exact=aw_of.get(side)):
1163:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_WITHOUT_PARENT_VALUE"
1164:     return None
1: #!/usr/bin/env python
2: # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
3: """父输入兼容性 / 来源维度的唯一依据：核证登记表（生产与自检共用）。
4: 
5: 第八轮裁决 C 项明确否掉两条自动判据：
6: - **同文档不能自动证明兼容**——同一报告可能含调整前后列或混合来源；
7: - **上游「双否」不能直接证明兼容**——三协电机已证明 verdict 可能与原文相反，
8:   它只能作为待核线索。
9: 同时澄清：正常同口径累计数相减，**不要求两期调整额相等**；只有主张
10: 「调整影响相互抵消」时才须证明调整额相等。
11: 
12: 第九轮裁决 C 项又指出：只比对「父终值」不够——登记里把父文档换成无关文档、
13: 把父期间和口径写错、审查人填 `x`、日期留空，仍能返回 VERIFIED。因此把
14: 登记记录绑定到**实际父输入**（doc/period/value/口径三元组/版本）。
15: 
16: 第十轮裁决 B 项继续指出四处「只验形式」：
17: 
18: 1. **单位折算键名错误**。`_expect()` 提供 `unit_scale`，`gap()` 却读
19:    `expect[side].get("unit")`，实际恒回退 1 倍——真实原件
20:    `187,875.40 万元 = 1,878,754,000 元` 会被正确引文拒绝，反之 1 倍口径的
21:    错误引文会被放行。V10 统一改读 `unit_scale`，并限定金额字段只接受
22:    `1/1000/10000` 三档。
23: 2. **栏目与版本只验形式**。`p1_column` 任意非空即过、`adjust_version` 只验枚举
24:    与两父相等。V10 要求：栏目必须是**期间列头**且出现在引文中；版本必须等于
25:    该父实际采用的 `version_used` 归类（ORIGINAL / RESTATED），并在引文里有
26:    对应标记（重述后 / 无调整后列）。
27: 3. **引文含同一个数字不证明是目标指标和目标期间**。V10 要求引文同时含
28:    **指标名 + 期间列头 + 父数**，三者缺一不可。
29: 4. **生产引文源仍是上游摘要**。V10 的 `evidence_of(doc, page)` 必须回读
30:    **登记页**原件（生产侧由 `_pdf_page` 提供），不再是 `results.csv.evidence`。
31: 
32: 第十轮裁决 E 项：来源维度登记还须核对 `source_page`，引文须绑定具体表格／
33: 期间列头／目标行列，不能只靠「全文命中 + 含年份」——`Ltd.年度报告——2025——`
34: 这类封面片段曾同时取得 `COMPARATIVE_SCOPE` 与 `QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE`。
35: """
36: import csv
37: import datetime
38: import re
39: from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
40: from pathlib import Path
41: 
42: import evidence_scope as es
43: 
44: # ---------------- 父输入核证登记表 ----------------
45: COLS = ["security_id", "field_id", "period_key", "ytd_field",
46:         "p1_period", "p2_period", "p1_doc", "p2_doc",
47:         "p1_value", "p2_value", "p1_page", "p2_page",
48:         "p1_quote", "p2_quote",
49:         "p1_column", "p2_column", "p1_adjust_version", "p2_adjust_version",
50:         "consolidation_scope", "unit_scale", "currency", "scope_basis",
51:         "version_basis", "conclusion", "review_role", "reviewer", "reviewed_at"]
52: 
53: # 调整前后版本：登记只能声明这两类，且必须等于该父实际 version_used 的归类。
54: # 第十轮裁决 B2：不再允许 SAME_VERSION 作为免证标签——「两父同版本」由实际
55: # version_used 相等直接判定，不需要登记者另立名目。
56: ADJUST_VERSIONS = {"ORIGINAL", "RESTATED"}
57: REVIEW_ROLES = {"INDEPENDENT_REVIEW", "SELF_CHECK"}
58: REVIEWER_PLACEHOLDERS = {"", "x", "xx", "xxx", "-", "--", "n/a", "na",
59:                          "none", "null", "test", "tbd", "todo", "self", "me"}
60: # 金额字段允许的折算倍数（s00 的 unit_scale 实际取值：1 / 1000 / 10000，
61: # 另有 pct / times 用于比率字段——那些不参与父输入相减）
62: AMOUNT_UNITS = {"1", "1000", "10000"}
63: _AMOUNT_TOKEN = re.compile(r"(?<![\d.,])-?\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?")
64: _MIN_QUOTE = 12
65: _MIN_BASIS = 20
66: 
67: # 指标名：引文必须点出目标指标（目标行），否则「同数不同指标」无法区分
68: METRIC_ALIASES = {
69:     "FIN.REV.YTD": ("营业收入", "营业总收入"),
70:     "FIN.COST.YTD": ("营业成本", "营业总成本"),
71:     "FIN.NPDEDT.YTD": ("扣除非经常性损益", "扣非"),
72:     "FIN.NPP.YTD": ("归属于上市公司股东的净利润", "归属于母公司股东的净利润",
73:                     "归母净利润"),
74:     "FIN.OCF.YTD": ("经营活动产生的现金流量净额", "经营活动现金流量净额"),
75: }
76: # 版本标记：重述版必须在引文里出现「调整后/重述后/更正后」之一；
77: # 原版必须在引文里不出现这些标记（否则引的其实是调整后列）
78: _VER_MARK_RESTATED = ("调整后", "重述后", "更正后", "调整后金额")
79: _VER_MARK_FORBIDDEN_ORIGINAL = ("调整后", "重述后", "更正后")
80: # 期间列头须含年份或这些相对列名之一

3: """第十九轮 D1/D2/D3 反例的回归测试（不写任何交付物）。

FILE step4_column_witness.py
20: """
21: import csv
22: import io
23: import sys
24: from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
25: from pathlib import Path
26: 
27: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
28: sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
29: 
30: import parent_verify as pv          # noqa: E402
31: 
32: OUT = HERE
33: ORIG = Path("D:/StockResearchLab/IndustryInflectionLab/robotics_data/collection/originals")
34: Y_TOL = 4.0
35: EDGE_TOL = 1.5          # 右边界聚类容差（pt）
36: _HEADER_HINT = pv._COL_PERIOD_HINT
37: _TITLE = pv._TITLE_ROW
38: 
39: 
40: def _pdf(sec, doc):
41:     return next((p for p in (ORIG / sec.split(":")[-1]).rglob(doc + ".pdf")), None)
42: 
43: 
44: def _dec(txt):
45:     s = (txt or "").strip().replace(",", "")
46:     if not s or not s.replace(".", "").replace("-", "").isdigit():
47:         return None
48:     try:
49:         return Decimal(s)
50:     except InvalidOperation:
51:         return None
52: 
53: 
54: def _rows(words, tol=Y_TOL):
55:     """独立实现的行聚类：[(y, [(x0, x1, text)])]，按 y 升序。"""
56:     rows = []
57:     for x0, y0, x1, _y1, tx in sorted(words, key=lambda w: (round(w[1], 1), w[0])):
58:         if rows and abs(y0 - rows[-1][0]) <= tol:
59:             rows[-1][1].append((x0, x1, tx))
60:         else:
61:             rows.append([y0, [(x0, x1, tx)]])
62:     return [(y, sorted(c)) for y, c in rows]
63: 
64: 
65: def _column_grid(rows, skip_ys):
66:     """整页数字的右边界格：[(右边界, 该列最左数字的左边界, 词数)]。
67: 
68:     财务报表的数字右对齐，同一列的数字右边界几乎相同。右边界相同的数字里
69:     最靠左的那个，就是这一列数字实际占到的左边界——列的可视范围由此确定，
70:     不需要假设列宽。
71:     """
72:     items = []
73:     for y, cells in rows:
74:         if any(abs(y - s) <= Y_TOL for s in skip_ys):
75:             continue
76:         for x0, x1, tx in cells:
77:             if _dec(tx) is not None:
78:                 items.append((x1, x0))
79:     items.sort()
80:     grid = []
81:     for x1, x0 in items:
82:         if grid and x1 - grid[-1][0] <= EDGE_TOL:
83:             grid[-1][0] = max(grid[-1][0], x1)
84:             grid[-1][1] = min(grid[-1][1], x0)
85:             grid[-1][2] += 1
86:         else:
87:             grid.append([x1, x0, 1])
88:     return grid
89: 
90: 
91: def _column_span(grid, x1):
92:     """金额右边界落在哪一列，以及该列的可视横向范围。"""
93:     if not grid:
94:         return None, None
95:     idx = min(range(len(grid)), key=lambda i: abs(grid[i][0] - x1))
96:     if abs(grid[idx][0] - x1) > EDGE_TOL * 2:
97:         return None, None
98:     left = grid[idx][1] - 12.0
99:     right = grid[idx][0] + 6.0
100:     return idx, (left, right)

3511: def check_c41(rows, page_of=None, col_of_for=None):
3512:     """逐行回原件重推父输入登记。返回违例列表 [(sec, fld, pk, 原因)]。"""
3513:     bad = []
3514:     for r in rows:
3515:         sec = (r.get("security_id") or "").strip()
3516:         fld = (r.get("field_id") or "").strip()
3517:         pk = (r.get("period_key") or "").strip()
3518:         ytd = (r.get("ytd_field") or "").strip()
3519:         tag = (sec, fld, pk)
3520:         if not sec or not fld or not pk or ytd != fld[:-3] + ".YTD":
3521:             bad.append(tag + ("BINDING_INCOMPLETE",))
3522:             continue
3523:         if (r.get("conclusion") or "").strip() != "COMPATIBLE":
3524:             bad.append(tag + ("NOT_COMPATIBLE",))
3525:             continue
3526:         if not pv._reviewer_ok(r) or not pv._role_ok(r) or not pv._date_ok(r.get("reviewed_at")):
3527:             bad.append(tag + ("REVIEW_TRACE_MISSING",))
3528:             continue

---
name: "pdf"
description: "Read, create, inspect, render, and verify PDF files where visual layout matters, including fillable AcroForms. Use Poppler rendering plus Python tools such as reportlab, pdfplumber, and pypdf for generation and extraction."
---

# PDF Skill

## When To Use

- Read or review PDF content where layout and visuals matter.
- Create PDFs programmatically with reliable formatting.
- Fill and validate interactive PDF forms.
- Validate final rendering before delivery.

## Tools + Contract Requirements

Immediately before the first create/edit authoring command, run `mark_artifact_operation_started.mjs` successfully exactly once using the command below. Do not run it for read-only work. For edits, replace `create` with `edit`; adjust the expected count and output format to match the requested outputs.



```bash
node container_tools/mark_artifact_operation_started.mjs --operation-kind create --expected-output-count 1 --output-format pdf
```

## Workflow

1. Prefer visual review: render PDF pages to PNGs and inspect them.
   - Use `pdftoppm` from the bundled runtime or system Poppler when available.
   - If unavailable, install Poppler or ask the user to review the output locally.
2. Use `reportlab` to generate PDFs when creating new documents.
3. Use `pdfplumber` or `pypdf` for text extraction and quick checks; do not rely on text extraction for layout fidelity.
4. After each meaningful update, re-render pages and verify alignment, spacing, and legibility.

## Fill And Validate AcroForms

Visual review alone is not a correctness check for a fillable PDF. A page `/Widget` annotation can render a value from its appearance stream while the canonical `/AcroForm/Fields` tree is missing or contains a stale value.

1. Keep the result interactive by default; set `flatten=True` only when the user explicitly requests a completed, static form. Preserve the source PDF, and do not flatten a signed PDF without an explicit workflow decision.
2. Inspect both representations before filling: enumerate fields from `reader.get_fields()` and `/Widget` annotations from every page's `/Annots`, following `/Parent` and `/Kids`. If a widget and a canonical field have the same name but are distinct objects with no `/Parent` relationship, do not call `reattach_fields()` blindly: it can create a second top-level field with the same name. Report the ambiguity or produce a static result.
3. Recover genuinely orphaned widgets, fill all pages, and write the result with `pypdf`:

```python
from pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter
from pypdf.generic import NameObject

reader = PdfReader(input_pdf)
writer = PdfWriter()
writer.clone_document_from_reader(reader)

# Restores widgets that are missing from /AcroForm/Fields.
writer.reattach_fields()
fields = writer.get_fields() or {}
missing = set(expected_values) - set(fields)
if missing:
    raise ValueError(f"Form fields not found after repair: {sorted(missing)}")

values_to_write = dict(expected_values)
if flatten:
    # Paint every existing value before removing every widget.
    values_to_write = {
        name: field.get("/V", "/Off" if field.get("/FT") == "/Btn" else "")
        for name, field in fields.items()
    }
    values_to_write.update(expected_values)

writer.update_page_form_field_values(
    None, values_to_write, auto_regenerate=False, flatten=flatten
)

if flatten:
    # pypdf's flatten=True paints appearances but does not remove widgets.
    writer.remove_annotations(subtypes="/Widget")
    writer.root_object.pop(NameObject("/AcroForm"), None)

with open(output_pdf, "wb") as stream:
    writer.write(stream)
```

4. Reopen the written PDF before delivery. For an interactive result, require every expected field to be present in `get_fields()` with the expected `/V`, enumerate page widgets again, and confirm their effective `/V` (the widget value or inherited `/Parent` value) agrees. Confirm each updated widget has a non-empty `/AP` `/N` appearance and render the final pages to catch stale or clipped appearances. Do not rely on `/NeedAppearances` or a successful PNG render as proof that logical field data was updated.
5. For a flattened result, require zero `/Widget` annotations and no remaining `/AcroForm` field tree after reopening, then render the final pages. Keep an editable copy when the user may need to revise the form.

## Temp And Output Conventions

- Use `tmp/pdfs/` for intermediate files; delete them when done.
- Write final artifacts under `output/pdf/` when working in this repo.
- Keep filenames stable and descriptive.

## Dependencies

Prefer the Codex bundled workspace/runtime dependencies when available. The primary runtime is expected to include:

- Python packages: `reportlab`, `pdfplumber`, `pypdf`
- Rendering tools: `pdftoppm` and `pdfinfo` from Poppler

If a dependency is missing, install only what is needed.

Python packages:

```bash
uv pip install reportlab pdfplumber pypdf
```

If `uv` is unavailable:

```bash
python3 -m pip install reportlab pdfplumber pypdf
```

System tools for rendering:

```bash
# macOS (Homebrew)
brew install poppler

# Ubuntu/Debian
sudo apt-get install -y poppler-utils
```

If installation is not possible in this environment, tell the user which dependency is missing and how to install it locally.

## Environment

No required environment variables.

## Rendering Command

```bash
pdftoppm -png "$INPUT_PDF" "$OUTPUT_PREFIX"
```

## Quality Expectations

- Maintain polished visual design: consistent typography, spacing, margins, and section hierarchy.
- Avoid rendering issues: clipped text, overlapping elements, broken tables, black squares, or unreadable glyphs.
- Charts, tables, and images must be sharp, aligned, and clearly labeled.
- Use ASCII hyphens only. Avoid U+2011 and other Unicode dashes.
- Citations and references must be human-readable; never leave tool tokens or placeholder strings.

## Final Checks

- Do not deliver until the latest PNG inspection shows zero visual or formatting defects.
- Confirm headers, footers, page numbering, and section transitions look polished.
- Keep intermediate files organized or remove them after final approval.

## Final response

### Final Response Citations

- **Create/edit:** for created or edited PDFs, cite each final deliverable exactly once with `purpose="output"`, in its own paragraph. Briefly describe the result without repeating its filename, path, or link. Never put output directives inside prose or list items.
- **Q&A and summaries:** Every file whose contents you summarize or use to support an answer MUST receive a `:codex-file-citation{...}` with `purpose="source"` in the final response. Use the source citation in place of the filename, wherever the filename would appear. Do not write the filename separately and append its citation at the end. Files merely inspected but not used in the answer need no citation. Do not emit output citations for unchanged files or read-only answers.

- Emit `:codex-file-citation{...}` directly, without backticks, code fences, or a Markdown-link wrapper. Use verified targets only. No need to cite or reference intermediate work.

For file citations, set `path` to the verified absolute file path on the task host. Omit `mode`.

Some illustrative examples, real responses use verified targets outside code fences.

```text
:codex-file-citation{path="/absolute/path/review.pdf" purpose="output"}
```

```text
The review of :codex-file-citation{path="/absolute/path/review.pdf" purpose="source"} is due Friday.
```


### Additional Citation Guidance

For PDF Q&A, inspect complete relevant pages and preserve material headings, table/figure labels, footnotes, sources, and sample sizes. PDF citations support only plain file citations; do not add `artifact_kind`, `page_number`, or other locators.

210: 
211: **代价（如实报，也是本轮唯一的覆盖率损失）**：23 条 `BLOCKED` 里，
212: 有 **12 个侧实例**的 note 含 `TABLE_BOUNDARY_UNPROVEN`（p1 6 个、p2 6 个）。
213: 它们**全部同时**已有 `NO_LABEL_ROW` 或 `NO_AMOUNT`，即边界收紧没有单独把任何一条
214: 原本可判的记录打成待核。**但覆盖率上限仍未量化**：真实页面里若表头与数据之间夹了
215: `单位：元` 或 `续` 字样，会退化为待核。这是方向安全的漏取，不是误收。
216: 
217: ### 6. `targets()`：固定重验基线
218: 
219: **你指出的错**（复现无误）：`targets()` 读的是**已缩到 64 行的当前登记表**，
220: 用当前文件重算只得 **86** 条，漏 `SH:600143 FIN.OCF.SQ@20260630`。
221:

223: 
224: - 新增 `step4_parent_targets.csv`（**新文件**）：**87 键固定 assignment**，
225:   含 `frozen_at` 与 `frozen_note`，一次性冻结，不再从登记表反推。
226: - `step4_parent_scan.py:155 load_target_baseline()` 读该文件；
227:   `targets()`（`:172`）的 keep 集合改为「**不可变基线 ∪ 当前登记键**」；

- 指定的上下两行冲突期间返回 `AMBIGUOUS`；横排互补片段正确合并，canon 为 `本期|R0-1-6|累计`。
- 两组相对期间冲突均判 False；2025／2026 年度保留年份。指定金额、引文反例及两个 `gap()` 拒绝状态映射均通过；独立运行 `selftest_column_rules.py` 为零失败。
- 单侧版本依据、单期间口径依据均被拒绝。C41 产物中十二类各 **64/64**、`swap_column` **32/32**，对应分母一致。
- 实际调用 `targets()` 得 **87 个唯一键**，包含金发科技指定 OCF 键，`MISSING_FROM_SCOPE=[]`，覆盖旧 70 键。
- 汇总计数和 L2 **43／231／321／81／378** 均吻合；两份历史报告已就地勘误。

**逐键比对也成立，但须纠正比较基线。**当前 `HEAD=b4738b2` 已是本轮提交，与工作区零差异。以真正上轮 `4ac102d` 比较：87 键双向差集为空、verdict 变化 0、侧状态恰好变化 1 条；登记表为 **64×27**，唯一变化字段确为全部 64 行的 `version_basis`。这证明登记结论未改，不能单独证明判定方法充分。

需要完成以下四项返工，文件均在被复核目录内。

1. **`parent_verify.py::_header_cells / _chain_cells`：互补语义不能单独证明属于同一列。**

   我独立构造两列数字：

   ```text
   表头 y=70：本报告期 x[100,130]；（4-6月）x[134,165]
   金额 y=90：100.00  x[100,130]；200.00   x[140,170]
   ```

   生产和旁证均返回 `OK`，并把第一列认成 `本报告期（4-6月）`。

   **验收方向：**合并前利用数字列格、相邻列边界检查片段归属；片段分属不同列或无法证明同列时，返回歧义／待核。保留真实同列断词的正例。

   对 **Q1**：不接受“真实财报不会这样排”的推断；也不指定未经校准的“金额宽度＋N pt”。短数字与宽表头并不支持统一宽度阈值，应按相邻列边界约束。

2. **`parent_verify.py::cols_equivalent / gap`、`verify_v5.py::check_c41`：堵住同侧登记凭空增加期间语义。**

   独立夹具中，目标金额实际表头为 `本报告期`，邻列表头为 `本报告期（1-6月）`。把登记列名写成邻列的长名称后：

   ```text
   生产取列：本报告期，OK
   gap()：None
   check_c41()：[]
   ```

   长名称确实在同页连续出现，但属于邻列，现有检查仍放行。

   **验收方向：**将“跨文档名称兼容”和“同页实际金额列身份”分开。同侧登记不得增加实际列尚未证明的区间、年份或累计语义；补充信息必须绑定该金额所在列的表头／上下文。增加上述反例，经真实 `gap → C41` 检验拒绝。

   对 **Q2**：允许缺省信息用于片段兼容，不能把它当作同侧列身份已经证明。无需强迫 p1、p2 两份文档的列名完全一致。“43 条全灭”尚无可复算依据；我按 p1/p2 比较得到空／非空分量不一致 **63 行**，等价 **32 行**，而 `2026年半年度` 对 `本报告期` 实际为 False。请明确原统计的比较对象，逐侧重算影响。

3. **`step4_column_witness.py::witness`：在横向合并前检查冲突。**

   同一表头行放置：

   ```text
   2025年半年度 x[100,113]
   2026年半年度 x[114,130]
   ```

   生产返回 `AMBIGUOUS`，旁证却返回：

   ```text
   OK，column=2025年半年度2026年半年度
   ```

   原因是旁证先无条件合并间隙 ≤6pt 的词，之后两两 `_compatible` 已看不到内部冲突。

   **验收方向：**合并前检查原始片段语义，保留可追溯片段；内部冲突必须报歧义。补横向冲突、纵向冲突及互补正例。旁证仍与生产共享表头行和指标带假设，不能称完全独立。

4. **`report_to_codex_round21.md`、`rework_round20_result.json`：修正统计和解释。**

   - 23 条 BLOCKED 中，note 含边界状态的是 **21 侧：p1=9、p2=12**，不是 12 侧。其中六个 p2 的 note 只有边界诊断，但最终侧状态为 `OK`，须区分候选页失败与另页成功。
   - “本轮零新增 BLOCKED”由逐键比较支持；不能据此推导边界规则没有过度收紧。我抽查泰豪科技该原件第 9 页，旧函数为 `NO_HEADER_ROW`，新函数为 `TABLE_BOUNDARY_UNPROVEN`，属于失败原因细化。:codex-file-citation{path="D:/StockResearchLab/IndustryInflectionLab/robotics_data/collection/originals/600590/q3_2025/DOC-56CFBDE8A1EFBB18.pdf" purpose="source"}
   - 比较基线写明提交 SHA；重算并说明 Q2 的统计对象。C37 是新增检查共同覆盖生产链，并非四项各自都经过完整链。

其余问题的裁定：

- **Q3：接受。**`AMOUNT_PRECISION_INVALID` 直接拒绝取列，属于上轮允许的取严分支。
- **Q4：接受。**把既有文档号、期间追加到派生依据中属于显式绑定；须继续保留原引文，追加文字本身不构成新证据。
- **`swap_column`：接受重算后仍为 32。**但“与区间判据无关”的解释不准确，这些名称本身包含区间省略；应以生产函数重算结果为依据。
- **Q6：接受 append-only。**新增批次登记新增键及冻结时间／版本，旧行不覆盖；旧键更正另留版本追溯。

完成上述修复后重验固定 87 键，如实报告结果，不预设必须维持 64 条。**S04 第二轮、S05 继续不放行；43／231／378 不冻结。**本轮只读复核，未修改仓库，也未重跑会写入产物的整套脚本。
